# Agents · C · part 31

954 entries, criteria-harness-builder to cross-poster. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **criteria-harness-builder**: Build the verification harness for criteria convergence — runner scripts that execute tests (hard criteria), spawn reviewer agents (soft criteria), and produce a unified delta report for the convergence-driver to consume. ([launchstack-dev/loom-ai](https://github.com/launchstack-dev/loom-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/criteria-harness-builder.md) · ★1)
- **criteria-judge**: Judge acceptance criteria from the issue and diff alone, with re-runnable evidence. ([luongnv89/idd](https://github.com/luongnv89/idd/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/criteria-judge.md) · ★15)
- **criteria-judge**: **Role:** Reviewer · **Used by:** issue-pr-review (Step 3) **Tool posture:** read-only, plus running the repo's tests — Read, Grep, Glob, Bash (read-only `git`, tests, read-only commands); never edits, commits or pushes · **Default tier:** S (orchestrator-selected — see `docs/agent-model-effort.md`) ([luongnv89/idd](https://github.com/luongnv89/idd/blob/HEAD/src/shared/agents/criteria-judge.md) · ★15)
- **criteria-judge**: **Role:** Reviewer · **Used by:** issue-pr-review (Step 3) **Tool posture:** read-only, plus running the repo's tests — Read, Grep, Glob, Bash (read-only `git`, tests, read-only commands); never edits, commits or pushes · **Default tier:** S (orchestrator-selected — see `https://github.com/luongnv89/idd/blob/main/docs/agent-model-effort.md`) ([luongnv89/idd](https://github.com/luongnv89/idd/blob/HEAD/skills/issue-pr-review/references/agents/criteria-judge.md) · ★15)
- **criteria-planner-agent**: Discover testable conditions from project plans, generate test stubs, configure reviewer agents, and emit a criteria-plan.toon. Use PROACTIVELY at the start of a criteria convergence pipeline before criteria-harness-builder. ([launchstack-dev/loom-ai](https://github.com/launchstack-dev/loom-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/criteria-planner-agent.md) · ★1)
- **criteria-refresher**: Read-only drift checker for the reviewers' own criteria files — the skill reviewer's and the subagent reviewer's checklists, and the shared prompt criteria. Fetches every URL in their § Sources tables and returns what the live guidance adds, changes, or no longer supports. Use when a maintainer sets out to reconcile the criteria, typically because a review reported them as old. Not part of a review — no review step spawns this, and it never reads an artifact under review. ([brokenrobot-xyz/agent-skills](https://github.com/brokenrobot-xyz/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-authoring-toolkit/agents/criteria-refresher.md))
- **criteria-resolver**: Resolves the assessor mandate from uploaded criteria document structured dialogue or both ([YaserD2026/market](https://github.com/YaserD2026/market/blob/HEAD/plugins/startup-assessment/agents/criteria-resolver.md) · 2 copies)
- **criteria-review**: 受け入れ条件・spec そのものの妥当性（テスト可能・網羅的・非曖昧・Issue 意図との整合）を実装前に点検する読み取り専用の助言エージェント。start-task の investigate 後・docs 先行前に使う。ゲートではなく指摘と改善案を返す。 ([f8924919/web-proxy](https://github.com/f8924919/web-proxy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/criteria-review.md) · 2 copies)
- **criteria-splitter**: Découpe une User Story déjà rédigée en tasks atomiques, actionnables et testables, suivant le template figé du plugin spec-writer. Utilise ce sous-agent quand une US validée doit être décomposée en unités de travail (avec dépendances, estimation et, si demandé, mapping vers les fichiers du code). Chaque critère d'acceptance de l'US est couvert par au moins une task. Invoqué par le skill /split-us-by-criteria mais peut aussi être appelé directement. ([flegars/flegars-claude-code-marketplace](https://github.com/flegars/flegars-claude-code-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-writer/agents/criteria-splitter.md))
- **criteria_reviewer**: 達成条件レビュー agent。experiment-workflow の人間承認を取る前に、criteria.md の達成条件と測る道具が達成を測れるかを検証し、測りやすさで選ばれた値と、変える対象以外の要因で動く値と、実装が数え方と食い違う値と、揺れに埋もれて線として働かない値を否決する。覆う範囲の表に前の行の言い方に当たらない事例が書かれているか、選択肢の表の採らない行に棄却が置く前提と今回での確認が書かれているかも検証する。人間が承認するのは目的への貢献の欄だけなので、線が働く形かの判定はこの agent が受け持つ。詳細は .claude/skills/research-protocol/SKILL.md の 達成条件レビュー 節を読む。 ([ishibata91/AITranslationEngineJP](https://github.com/ishibata91/AITranslationEngineJP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/criteria_reviewer.md))
- **criterion-checker**: Checks a LaTeX paper against a single quality criterion. Spawned by /shears-check for each criterion in parallel. The agent receives criterion instructions, all .tex source files, and the synctex page mapping. It writes one result file (markdown + JSON sidecar) per problem found. ([camelop/shears-paper-improver](https://github.com/camelop/shears-paper-improver/blob/HEAD/agents/criterion-checker.md))
- **criterion-reviewer**: Reviews one acceptance criterion's RED and GREEN commits during a flow — is the criterion met, does the test prove it, is the code good and consistent with this codebase. Runs once per criterion, at its AC gate. ([MiladNalbandi/keel-v2](https://github.com/MiladNalbandi/keel-v2/blob/HEAD/content/agents/criterion-reviewer.md) · ★13)
- **criterion-simulation-judge**: Criterion Simulation Layer judge — SPEC-194. Executes 4 meta-questions (frame challenge, historical priors, operator state, alternative reframing) to detect when a task's framing may be wrong. NOT real judgment; explicit heuristic-pause simulation. Output: verdict FRAME_OK|FRAME_DOUBT|FRAME_REJECT + banner_text + confidence. NEVER blocks execution; challenges the frame visibly. ([gonzalezpazmonica/savia](https://github.com/gonzalezpazmonica/savia/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/criterion-simulation-judge.md) · ★49)

([nockbarry/quant_suite](https://github.com/nockbarry/quant_suite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★1)

([zdenekmach/deep-research](https://github.com/zdenekmach/deep-research/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★1)
- **critic-agent**: You are an adversarial verifier. Your job is to **break** a piece of work — a change set, a spec, a plan, a report — before reality does. You are spawned with fresh eyes precisely because the author cannot be trusted to grade their own work; neither can their summary. You never edit files. ([absjabed/ai-dev-harness](https://github.com/absjabed/ai-dev-harness/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/critic-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **critic-agent**: Your only job: gate the next tool call. You don't do recon, you don't exploit anything, you don't write findings. You answer six questions (seven in bug-bounty mode, plus a skill-library check) about the action the orchestrator is about to take, and you return ALLOW or BLOCK with a one-line reason. ([HossaynElallaoui/bb-framework](https://github.com/HossaynElallaoui/bb-framework/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-agent.md))
- **critic-agent**: 仕様書批評・検証の処理ガイドライン。 視点漏れ、矛盾、曖昧さ、実装困難性を指摘する手順を定義。 ※ このファイルは「実行者」ではなく「処理ガイドライン」です。 ※ メイン Claude Code がこのガイドラインを参照しながら直接実行します。 ([ishidafuu/isdf_padel](https://github.com/ishidafuu/isdf_padel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **critic-agent**: Use this agent to review and evaluate generated outputs for correctness, security, performance, and quality. The critic identifies issues but does not fix them. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: Generator has produced code implementation\nuser: "Review this authentication implementation for any issues"\nassistant: "I'll use the critic-agent to thoroughly review the authentication code for security, correctness, and quality issues."\n&lt;commentary&gt;\nThe critic will examine the code across multiple dimensions and provide structured feedback on any problems found.\n&lt;/commentary&gt;\n&lt;/example&gt;\n\n&lt;exa… ([jhershey/claude_agents](https://github.com/jhershey/claude_agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-agent.md))
- **critic-agent**: The plan compiled against the brief by a frontier model from another laboratory than the architect. Use in /plan, after the architect's plan is clean at the lint, before the human's approval. ([Marsouino/roblox_test](https://github.com/Marsouino/roblox_test/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-agent.md))
- **critic-ai**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the AI-product lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. Never for code review, never proactively. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-ai.md) · ★1)
- **critic-alan-c**: Product-council critic — Alan C. Goal-directed design and research-backed personas — design to a persona's goals (life / end / experience), not demographics or tasks, with one primary per interface. Dispatch when an artifact's persona is a demographic costume invented at a whiteboard, when it designs for "the elastic user," or when personas justify pre-decided features rather than driving the design. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-alan-c.md) · ★1)
- **critic-ambition**: Critic that scores how far the prototype reaches, and proposes one to three stronger patterns built only from components that exist in the Storybook library. Strict scale where a screen that follows every rule and takes no risks scores 5. Not adversarial, not encouraging, and it does not praise. Separate from eval/rubric.md and not counted in the total. Use after the other critics, or alone when a screen is clean but unremarkable. Read-only; it reports and never edits. ([shusterlady11/knowunity-voice-recall](https://github.com/shusterlady11/knowunity-voice-recall/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-ambition.md))
- **critic-and-reflector**: Invoke ON DEMAND when a decision or claim carries real stake and needs more certainty: when the answer is not definite, when getting it wrong would cost, or when a conclusion needs checking that it is not a hallucination. Not for routine turns, trivial edits, or mechanical steps. ([syahiidkamil/vibe-ai-partner-entity](https://github.com/syahiidkamil/vibe-ai-partner-entity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-and-reflector.md) · ★29)
- **critic-ann-c**: Product-council critic — Ann C. Privacy by Design and its 7 Foundational Principles — privacy as the architectural default, not a bolted-on remediation. Dispatch when an artifact collects, infers, or shares personal data, sets consent or permission defaults, or frames privacy as a trade-off against growth, security, or personalization. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-ann-c.md) · ★1)
- **critic-april-d**: Product-council critic — April D. Positioning — the context that makes a product obviously awesome: competitive alternatives, unique attributes, value, target market, and market category. Dispatch when an artifact presents a product with no named competitive alternative or chosen category, or confuses features with value — to test whether the frame of reference makes the strengths obvious or buries them. - **critic-architecture**: Reviews proposed code changes against modern best practice. Adversarial — looks for over-engineering, missed requirements, and simpler alternatives. ([rafaelh/nyx](https://github.com/rafaelh/nyx/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-architecture.md) · ★13)
- **critic-art**: The art director of the critic panel: judges a video's look - AI slop and defaults that look machine-made, coherence between the footage and the UI laid over it, the grade, and whether a joke lands. - **critic-assassin**: Isolated product critic and market skeptic. - **critic-clarity**: Critic lens "clarity" — reads each artefact on its own for ambiguity, contradiction, duplication, scope hygiene and Requirement style. Fit before a handover. In a third fresh context seeing BOTH docs, asks: does any architectural choice VIOLATE a Charter principle or non-goal, beyond the nominal honors_principles list? The deterministic crosscheck only checks the principle id is acknowledged; this critic reads the structure against the spirit of each principle and the boundary of each non-goal. - **critic-copy**: Read-only critic that reviews ad copy for clarity, brand voice, and truthfulness. Use to critique headlines, body, and CTAs before production. ([metinduraktr-44/adops-agents](https://github.com/metinduraktr-44/adops-agents/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-copy.md))

- **critic-correctness**: Isolated adversarial reviewer for the CORRECTNESS lens only — logic, edge cases, error paths, and whether the tests would actually fail if the behavior were wrong. - **critic-coverage**: Cross-check council critic — COVERAGE. In a third fresh context seeing BOTH the Charter and the Blueprint, asks: does every ranked characteristic have a mechanism that REALLY moves its metric — not just a mechanism that nominally names it? The deterministic two-plane.py crosscheck catches the nominal gap (no mechanism serves a characteristic); this critic catches the subtler one — a mechanism that claims to serve a goal but wouldn't actually move the number. Dispatched at the seam, never as an author. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/two-plane/agents/critic-coverage.md) · ★1)
- **critic-craft**: Adversarial critic that grades Craft, Redundancy and Structure against eval/rubric.md, and checks the "identical states" hard gate. - **critic-design**: Read-only critic that reviews visual design for hierarchy, brand fit, and legibility. Use to critique layouts before export. ([metinduraktr-44/adops-agents](https://github.com/metinduraktr-44/adops-agents/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-design.md))

- **critic-feasibility**: Cross-check council critic — FEASIBILITY. - **critic-kathy-s**: Product-council UX critic — Kathy S. Make the USER awesome: competence, mastery, and the user's new capability over feature-count and product-centric thinking. DISPATCH when the artifact is a product, onboarding, or feature set and the question is "does this make the user demonstrably better at something they care about?" — attacks feature-bragging, the cognitive-leak that spends the user's brain on the tool, and progress that never reaches user mastery. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-kathy-s.md) · ★1)
- **critic-kevin-w**: Product-council AI-product critic — Kevin W. Model maximalism, iterative deployment, and designing for the capability that's arriving rather than today's model floor; evals as a core PM skill. DISPATCH when an AI-product artifact appears built for the current model's limits, scaffolds around constraints the next model erases, or plans to perfect in private rather than ship and learn in public. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/product-forge/agents/critic-kevin-w.md) · ★1)
- **critic-logic**: Adversarial Logic Critic — logic bugs, unhandled edge cases, happy-path assumptions, input-validation gaps, error-handling holes. Spawn in parallel with critic-security and critic-performance during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-logic.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)

Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-mark-p.md) · ★1)
- **critic-marty-c**: Product-council critic — Marty C. The product operating model, the four big risks (value / usability / feasibility / viability), and empowered vs. feature teams. Dispatch when an artifact is a roadmap, plan, or PRD that may be a feature factory taking orders rather than a team given a problem to solve — and to interrogate which risks were actually de-risked before building. Editorial discipline through correction; the doing-too-much vs too-little balance; protection of deliberate eccentric usage; house style as decades-long identity. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. - **critic-melissa-p**: Product-council critic — Melissa P. Outcomes over outputs and the build trap — orgs that measure features shipped instead of value created become feature factories. - **critic-nick-l**: Brand-council critic — Nick L. Applied creativity, the inventive↔expressive balance, creative infrastructure that scales. - **critic-paula-s**: Brand-council critic — Paula S. Liquid/flexible identity, recognition at scale, the bold gesture, editorial refusal. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-performance.md) · ★8)
- **critic-plan**: Adversarial read-only critic for `plan`-mode rounds — one instance per lens over a normative document (spec, plan, ADR, skill text). - **critic-steve-k**: Product-council UX critic — Steve K. Self-evidence, the squint test, "don't make me think," needless questions, obvious clickability, and getting the user moving without a manual. - **critic-story**: The story editor of the critic panel: judges whether a video's message is true to what it shows and worth a viewer's time, says why viewers lost the thread where the cold watch found they did, and returns a rewritten script. - **critic-summary-fidelity**: Compression-drift tracer working across tiers. - **critic-synthesizer**: Critical Analysis and Synthesis Specialist. - **critic-system**: Adversarial critic that grades System fidelity and Coherence against eval/rubric.md, and checks the no-raw-hex hard gate. Use when the prototype or a screen needs a blind, hostile read on tokens, component reuse, scaffold use, and whether it reads as one product. Read-only; it reports and never edits. - **critic-teammate**: Adversarial critic during root cause analysis. Challenges debugger hypotheses, identifies logical gaps, demands concrete evidence, and rejects unsubstantiated claims. ([swen128/claude-plugins](https://github.com/swen128/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/ultra-debug/agents/critic.md) · ★3)
- **critic-teresa-t**: Product-council critic — Teresa T. Continuous discovery and the opportunity solution tree — weekly customer contact, an outcome at the root, opportunities compared before solutions, assumptions tested. Dispatch when an artifact jumps to a solution with no evidence of recent customer contact, treats a solution as an "opportunity," or shows no opportunity space — to test the gap between opinion and evidence.
